Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in forecasting theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 200 and the invariant I(A,B) = 797 mod 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the forecasting decomposition. By minimality, the element a^200 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 797-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
